        /// <summary>
        /// Status of bit field when parsing. Options are: 0=IsAccepted, 1=IsFilled, 2=IsUpdated, 3=IsFinished. This is mostly for internal use
        /// </summary>
        public long? Status { get; init; } //Status

        /// <summary>

        /// <summary>
        /// Television network name of original broadcast
        /// </summary>
        public string OriginalNetworkName { get; init; } //OriginalNetworkName

        /// <summary>
        /// Country information of the content
        /// </summary>
        public string Country { get; init; } //Country

        /// <summary>
        /// Time zone information of the content
        /// </summary>
        public string TimeZone { get; init; } //TimeZone

        /// <summary>

        /// </summary>
        public string Cover { get; init; } //Cover

        /// <summary>
        /// Short description of cover image file (e.g. Earth in space)
        /// </summary>
        public string CoverDescription { get; init; } //Cover_Description

        /// <summary>
        /// Cover type (e.g. "Cover (front)")
        /// </summary>
        public string CoverType { get; init; } //Cover_Type

        /// <summary>

        /// </summary>
        public string CoverMime { get; init; } //Cover_Mime

        /// <summary>
        /// Cover, in binary format, encoded as Base64
        /// </summary>
        public string CoverData { get; init; } //Cover_Data

        /// <summary>
        /// Text of a song
        /// </summary>
        public string Lyrics { get; init; } //Lyrics

        /// <summary>

        /// </summary>
        public string Comment { get; init; } //Comment

        /// <summary>

        /// </summary>
        public string Rating { get; init; } //Rating

        /// <summary>
        /// Date/year the item was added to the owners collection
        /// </summary>
        public string AddedDate { get; init; } //Added_Date

        /// <summary>

        /// </summary>
        public string PlayedFirstDate { get; init; } //Played_First_Date

        /// <summary>

        /// </summary>

        /// <summary>

        /// </summary>
        public long PlayedCount { get; init; } //Played_Count

        /// </summary>
